On this page

Variational inference ​

Fit a hierarchical count model with brm_svi_fit, compare its Gaussian approximation with NUTS, continue the fit as new observations arrive, and combine fits on slices into a starting point. This example shows where the approximation loses population uncertainty even when its site-effect estimates agree with NUTS.

The model and the data ​

The synthetic data represent monitoring sites visited repeatedly. At each visit a count is recorded together with a covariate x, and the count rate of a site depends on x through the site's own intercept and slope. The two site effects are correlated. The data are a NamedTuple of nested vectors, one entry per site holding that site's visits; no table with one row per observation is needed.

julia
# The values the data are simulated from.
const GENERATING = (; a=1.0, b=0.4, sd_a=0.5, sd_b=0.25, cor_ab=0.3)

# `n_sites` sites, each visited `visits` times. Each site has its own intercept and
# slope, drawn together from a bivariate normal around the population values; the
# count at a visit is Poisson with log rate `a_i + b_i * x`. The data are a NamedTuple
# of nested vectors: one entry per site, holding that site's visits.
function simulate_sites(; n_sites=400, visits=8, seed=1, g=GENERATING)
    rng = Xoshiro(seed)
    C = [g.sd_a^2 g.cor_ab * g.sd_a * g.sd_b; g.cor_ab * g.sd_a * g.sd_b g.sd_b^2]
    L = cholesky(C).L
    effects = [L * randn(rng, 2) for _ in 1:n_sites]
    x = [randn(rng, visits) for _ in 1:n_sites]
    y = [[rand(rng, Poisson(exp(g.a + e[1] + (g.b + e[2]) * xj))) for xj in xi]
         for (e, xi) in zip(effects, x)]
    data = (; site=[string("s", i) for i in 1:n_sites], x, y)
    (; data, effects)
end

The model has a @plate over sites. The population part, a ~ 1 + (1 | s | site) and b ~ 1 + (1 | s | site), gives each site an intercept and a slope drawn from one correlated block s. The plate cell reads that site's two values and observes its counts.

brm-comparison
Correlated site intercepts and slopes for Poisson counts
panes: brm|stanblocks|rkppl|stan|jax:3
julia
function site_counts_model(data = site_counts_data())
    @brm data begin
        a ~ 1 + (1 | s | site)
        b ~ 1 + (1 | s | site)
        effect(a, Intercept) ~ Normal(0, 1)
        effect(b, Intercept) ~ Normal(0, 1)
        sd(:, s) ~ Exponential(1)
        @plate for i in eachindex(a)
            y[i] ~ Poisson(exp.(a[i] .+ b[i] .* x[i]))
        end
    end
end

ranef_correlated_draws_generic_configured_1 = Base.merge(BayesianRegressionModels.ranef_correlated_draws_generic, quote
            tau ~ (StanBlocks.builtin.restricted)(exponential, 1.0 ./ 1; n = n_terms, lower = 0.0)
        end)
emitted @slic body:
begin
    b_s_site ~ ranef_correlated_draws_generic_configured_1(; group_idx = site_idx, n_groups = n_site, n_terms = n_terms_s_site, lkj_eta = b_s_site_lkj_eta)
    X_a = hcat(rep_vector(1.0, num_elements(site_idx)))
    pop_a ~ _popefs_normal(; X = X_a, beta_loc = [0], beta_scale = [1])
    r_a_s_site = b_s_site[site_idx, 1]
    a = pop_a + r_a_s_site
    X_b = hcat(rep_vector(1.0, num_elements(site_idx)))
    pop_b ~ _popefs_normal(; X = X_b, beta_loc = [0], beta_scale = [1])
    r_b_s_site = b_s_site[site_idx, 2]
    b = pop_b + r_b_s_site
    y_cell ~ plate(a, y, b, x; outer = (kernel_nsub_y_cell,)) do a, y, b, x
            #= brm-docs-example.jl:9 =#
            y ~ poisson(exp.(a .+ b .* x))
            #= brm-docs-example.jl:10 =#
            nothing
        end
end
julia
"""
Group-level (varying) effects of grouping `g`, `K` correlated margins per level.

Non-centered, from the scales `tau[1:K]` (drawn from their priors), the Cholesky factor of the margins' correlation `L ~ LKJCholesky(K, eta)` and standardized effects
`z[1:length(levels(g)), 1:K] ~ Normal(0, 1)`. Returns the
level-by-margin matrix `z * transpose(tau .* L)`, one row per level of `g` in order.
"""
brm_correlated_group_effects(g, K, eta) = begin
        tau[1:K] .~ Exponential.(1)
        L ~ LKJCholesky(K, eta)
        z[1:length(levels(g)), 1:K] .~ Normal.(0, 1)
        return z * transpose(tau .* L)
    end

begin
    site_fit_levels = brm_fit_levels(site_fit)
    b_s_site ~ brm_correlated_group_effects(site_fit_levels, 2, 1.0)
    site_level = brm_level_indices(site, site_fit_levels)
    ranef_a_s_site = b_s_site[site_level, 1]
    ranef_b_s_site = b_s_site[site_level, 2]
    a_Intercept ~ Normal(0.0, 1.0)
    a = a_Intercept .+ ranef_a_s_site
    b_Intercept ~ Normal(0.0, 1.0)
    b = b_Intercept .+ ranef_b_s_site
    @plate for i = eachindex(x)
            y_cell_argument_y_1[i] = exp.(a[i] .+ b[i] .* x[i])
        end
    @plate for i = eachindex(y)
            y[i] .~ Poisson.(y_cell_argument_y_1[i])
        end
end
stan
functions {
matrix hcat(vector x) {
    int n = dims(x)[1];
    return to_matrix(x, n, 1);
}
vector poisson_lpmfs(
    array[] int obs,
    vector lambda
) {
    return jbroadcasted_poisson_lpmfs(obs, lambda);
}
vector jbroadcasted_poisson_lpmfs(
    array[] int x1,
    vector x2
) {
    int n = dims(x1)[1];
    vector[n] rv;
    for(i in 1:n) {
        rv[i] = poisson_lpmfs(broadcasted_getindex(x1, i), broadcasted_getindex(x2, i));
    }
    return rv;
}
real poisson_lpmfs(int args1, real args2) {
    return poisson_lpmf(args1 | args2);
}
int broadcasted_getindex(array[] int x, int i) {
    return x[i];
}
real broadcasted_getindex(vector x, int i) {
    return x[i];
}
array[] int poisson_int_rng(
    int anontok__1,
    vector p
) {
    int n = anontok__1;
    return poisson_rng(p);
}
int ragged_end(array[] int ends, int i) {
    return ends[i];
}
int ragged_start(
    array[] int ends,
    int i
) {
    if((i == 1)) {
        return 1;
    } else {
        return (1 + ends[(i - 1)]);
    }
}
vector jbroadcasted_exp(
    vector x1
) {
    int n = dims(x1)[1];
    vector[n] rv;
    for(i in 1:n) {
        rv[i] = exp(broadcasted_getindex(x1, i));
    }
    return rv;
}
}
data {
    int n_terms_s_site;
    real b_s_site_lkj_eta;
    int n_site;
    int site_idx_n;
    array[site_idx_n] int site_idx;
    int y_mem_n;
    int y_ends_n;
    tuple(array[y_mem_n] int, array[y_ends_n] int) y;
    int kernel_nsub_y_cell;
    int x_ends_n;
    int x_mem_n;
    tuple(vector[x_mem_n], array[x_ends_n] int) x;
}
transformed data {
    matrix[num_elements(site_idx), 1] X_a = hcat(rep_vector(1.0, num_elements(site_idx)));
    int pop_a_n_covariates = 1;
    matrix[num_elements(site_idx), 1] X_b = hcat(rep_vector(1.0, num_elements(site_idx)));
    int pop_b_n_covariates = 1;
}
parameters {
    cholesky_factor_corr[n_terms_s_site] b_s_site_L;
    vector<lower=0.0>[n_terms_s_site] b_s_site_tau;
    vector[(n_terms_s_site * n_site)] b_s_site_z_flat;
    vector[pop_a_n_covariates] pop_a_beta_pop;
    vector[pop_b_n_covariates] pop_b_beta_pop;
}
transformed parameters {
    matrix[n_terms_s_site, n_site] b_s_site_z = to_matrix(b_s_site_z_flat, n_terms_s_site, n_site);
    matrix[n_site, n_terms_s_site] b_s_site = ((diag_pre_multiply(b_s_site_tau, b_s_site_L) * b_s_site_z)');
    vector[num_elements(site_idx)] pop_a = (X_a * pop_a_beta_pop);
    vector[site_idx_n] r_a_s_site = b_s_site[site_idx, 1];
    vector[num_elements(site_idx)] a = (pop_a + r_a_s_site);
    vector[num_elements(site_idx)] pop_b = (X_b * pop_b_beta_pop);
    vector[site_idx_n] r_b_s_site = b_s_site[site_idx, 2];
    vector[num_elements(site_idx)] b = (pop_b + r_b_s_site);
}
model {
    b_s_site_L ~ lkj_corr_cholesky(b_s_site_lkj_eta);
    b_s_site_tau ~ exponential((1.0 ./ 1));
    b_s_site_z_flat ~ std_normal();
    pop_a_beta_pop ~ normal([0]', [1]');
    pop_b_beta_pop ~ normal([0]', [1]');
    for(plate_i__pl_1 in 1:kernel_nsub_y_cell) {
        y.1[ragged_start(y.2, plate_i__pl_1):ragged_end(y.2, plate_i__pl_1)] ~ poisson(
            jbroadcasted_exp(
                (
                    a[plate_i__pl_1] +
                    (b[plate_i__pl_1] .* x.1[ragged_start(x.2, plate_i__pl_1):ragged_end(x.2, plate_i__pl_1)])
                )
            )
        );
    }
}
generated quantities {
    array[num_elements(y.1)] int y_gen;
    vector[num_elements(y.2)] y_likelihood;
    for(plate_i__pl_1 in 1:kernel_nsub_y_cell) {
        y_gen[ragged_start(y.2, plate_i__pl_1):ragged_end(y.2, plate_i__pl_1)] = poisson_int_rng(
            (1 + (ragged_end(y.2, plate_i__pl_1) - ragged_start(y.2, plate_i__pl_1))),
            jbroadcasted_exp(
                (
                    a[plate_i__pl_1] +
                    (b[plate_i__pl_1] .* x.1[ragged_start(x.2, plate_i__pl_1):ragged_end(x.2, plate_i__pl_1)])
                )
            )
        );
        y_likelihood[plate_i__pl_1] = poisson_lpmf(y.1[ragged_start(y.2, plate_i__pl_1):ragged_end(y.2, plate_i__pl_1)] | 
            jbroadcasted_exp(
                (
                    a[plate_i__pl_1] +
                    (b[plate_i__pl_1] .* x.1[ragged_start(x.2, plate_i__pl_1):ragged_end(x.2, plate_i__pl_1)])
                )
            )
        );
    }
}

python
import jax
import model as m

prepared = m.prepare_data(**{k: inputs[k] for k in m.DATA})
lp = m.log_density(u, **prepared)               # u: unconstrained, length m.dimension(prepared)
lp = jax.jit(m.log_density)(u, **prepared)      # one compiled function for every dataset of these shapes
python
"""Generated by ReactiveKernelsPPL.jax_source from a built @rkppl program; do not edit.

Data: site, site_fit, x, y.

Model documentation:

- `b_s_site`: Group-level (varying) effects of grouping `g`, `K` correlated margins per level.
  
  Non-centered, from the scales `tau[1:K]` (drawn from their priors), the Cholesky factor of the margins' correlation `L ~ LKJCholesky(K, eta)` and standardized effects
  `z[1:length(levels(g)), 1:K] ~ Normal(0, 1)`. Returns the
  level-by-margin matrix `z * transpose(tau .* L)`, one row per level of `g` in order."""
import numpy as np
import scipy.special as ssp
from typing import Any

import jax
import jax.numpy as jnp
import jax.scipy.linalg
import jax.scipy.special as jsp
from jax.typing import ArrayLike

jax.config.update("jax_enable_x64", True)

DATA = {"site": {"dtype": "object", "ndim": 1, "ragged": False}, "site_fit": {"dtype": "object", "ndim": 1, "ragged": False}, "x": {"dtype": "float64", "ndim": 1, "ragged": True}, "y": {"dtype": "int64", "ndim": 1, "ragged": True}}
INPUTS = ("unconstrained", "ppl_last_b_s_site_z", "ppl_axis1_b_s_site_z", "site_level", "plate_argument", "x", "x_lengths", "x_flat", "y", "y_lengths", "y_flat")
PREPARED = {"ppl_last_b_s_site_z": {"dtype": "int64", "ndim": 0, "ragged": False}, "ppl_axis1_b_s_site_z": {"dtype": "int64", "ndim": 0, "ragged": False}, "site_level": {"dtype": "int64", "ndim": 1, "ragged": False}, "plate_argument": {"dtype": "int64", "ndim": 1, "ragged": False}, "x": {"dtype": "float64", "ndim": 1, "ragged": True}, "y": {"dtype": "int64", "ndim": 1, "ragged": True}}
UNTRANSLATED = {}


def _rk_order_key_np(x):
    # Julia's isless order of an array as sortable keys: a float is an integer
    # key, -0.0 before 0.0 and every NaN last, equal for values isequal treats
    # as equal. Integer and string arrays are their own keys.
    x = np.asarray(x)
    if x.dtype.kind != "f":
        return x
    bits = np.where(np.isnan(x), np.nan, x).astype(np.float64).view(np.int64)
    return np.where(bits < 0, bits ^ np.int64(0x7FFFFFFFFFFFFFFF), bits)


def _rk_unique_np(x):
    # Julia's unique of a vector: the first value of each isequal class, in
    # order.
    x = np.asarray(x)
    _, first = np.unique(_rk_order_key_np(x), return_index=True)
    return x[np.sort(first)]


def _rk_sort_np(x):
    # Julia's sort of a vector: ascending in isless order, equal values in
    # their order.
    x = np.asarray(x)
    return x[np.argsort(_rk_order_key_np(x), kind="stable")]


def _brm_sorted_labels_np(source):
    labels = _rk_unique_np(source)
    sorted_ = _rk_sort_np(labels)
    return sorted_


def _brm_fit_levels_np(source):
    value = _brm_sorted_labels_np(source)
    levels = value
    return levels


def _brm_level_indices_np(labels, source):
    value = _brm_fit_levels_np(source)
    levels = value
    lookup = {}
    for k in range(1, len(levels) + 1):
        lookup[levels[k - 1]] = k
    indices = np.zeros((len(labels),), dtype=np.int64)
    for r in range(1, len(labels) + 1):
        position = lookup.get(labels[r - 1], 0)
        if position == 0:
            raise ValueError("".join([str("RK backend: group label `"), str(labels[r - 1]), str("` is not one of the "), str(len(levels)), str(" fitted levels. A replay keeps the fitted levels of every grouping it does not resample; name the grouping in `resample_groups` to take the new data's own levels.")]))
        indices[r - 1] = position
    return indices


def _rk_levels_np(x):
    arr = np.asarray(x)
    values = sorted(set(v for v in arr.tolist() if v is not None))
    return np.array(values, dtype=object if arr.dtype == object else arr.dtype)


class StaticInt(int):
    """An integer of the prepared data that sets a shape.

    It is an `int` everywhere, and `jax.jit` treats it as static, so the
    functions jit with the prepared data as arguments; a new value traces
    them again.
    """

    def __repr__(self):
        return "StaticInt(%d)" % int(self)


jax.tree_util.register_pytree_node(StaticInt, lambda s: ((), int(s)), lambda v, _: StaticInt(v))


def _rk_pad_rows(rows, dtype):
    # A ragged value as the functions take it: the padded array (each row
    # repeats its last entry; an empty row is zeros), the lengths, and the
    # positions of the valid entries in the flattened padded array, whose
    # length is the total. `dtype` is the element type the program declares,
    # so an empty row (`[]`) keeps an integer column integer.
    rows = [np.asarray(r, dtype=dtype) for r in rows]
    lengths = np.asarray([r.shape[0] for r in rows], dtype=np.int64)
    width = max([1] + [int(n) for n in lengths])
    padded = np.stack([np.pad(r, (0, width - r.shape[0]), mode="edge") if r.shape[0] > 0
                       else np.zeros(width, dtype=dtype) for r in rows]) if rows else np.zeros((0, width), dtype=dtype)
    flat = np.flatnonzero(np.arange(width)[None, :] < lengths[:, None])
    return padded, lengths, flat


def prepare_data(site: Any, site_fit: Any, x: list, y: list) -> dict:
    """The functions' data inputs, computed from the raw data once per dataset.

    Pass the data by the names the model reads them; a list of arrays is a ragged
    column. Call the other functions with the result: `f(u, **prepared)`.
    """
    site = np.asarray(site, dtype=object)
    site_fit = np.asarray(site_fit, dtype=object)
    x = [np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64) for a in x]
    y = [np.asarray(a, dtype=np.int64) for a in y]
    value = _brm_fit_levels_np(site_fit)
    site_fit_levels = value
    value_2 = _brm_level_indices_np(site, site_fit_levels)
    site_level = np.asarray(value_2, dtype=np.int64)
    ppl_axis1_b_s_site_z = len(_rk_levels_np(site_fit_levels))
    ppl_last_b_s_site_z = 5 + 2 * ppl_axis1_b_s_site_z
    plate_argument = np.arange(1, len(x) + 1)
    x_padded, x_lengths, x_flat = _rk_pad_rows(x, np.float64)
    y_padded, y_lengths, y_flat = _rk_pad_rows(y, np.int64)
    return {"ppl_last_b_s_site_z": StaticInt(ppl_last_b_s_site_z), "ppl_axis1_b_s_site_z": StaticInt(ppl_axis1_b_s_site_z), "site_level": np.asarray(site_level, dtype=np.int64), "plate_argument": np.asarray(plate_argument, dtype=np.int64), "x": x_padded, "x_lengths": x_lengths, "x_flat": x_flat, "y": y_padded, "y_lengths": y_lengths, "y_flat": y_flat}


def log_density(unconstrained: ArrayLike, **prepared: ArrayLike) -> jax.Array:
    """The log density a sampler targets at `unconstrained`, the packed unconstrained parameters (length `dimension(prepared)`), for the data `prepare_data` returned: `f(u, **prepared)`.

    The sum of `log_prior`, `log_likelihood` and `log_jacobian`.
    """
    ppl_last_b_s_site_z = prepared["ppl_last_b_s_site_z"]
    ppl_axis1_b_s_site_z = prepared["ppl_axis1_b_s_site_z"]
    site_level = prepared["site_level"]
    plate_argument = prepared["plate_argument"]
    x = prepared["x"]
    x_lengths = prepared["x_lengths"]
    x_flat = prepared["x_flat"]
    y = prepared["y"]
    y_lengths = prepared["y_lengths"]
    y_flat = prepared["y_flat"]
    unconstrained = jnp.asarray(unconstrained)
    site_level = jnp.asarray(site_level)
    plate_argument = jnp.asarray(plate_argument)
    x = jnp.asarray(x)
    x_lengths = jnp.asarray(x_lengths)
    x_flat = jnp.asarray(x_flat)
    y = jnp.asarray(y)
    y_lengths = jnp.asarray(y_lengths)
    y_flat = jnp.asarray(y_flat)
    ppl_prior_parameters_normal_mu = jnp.asarray([0.0, 0.0], dtype=jnp.float64)
    ppl_prior_parameters_normal_sd = jnp.asarray([1.0, 1.0], dtype=jnp.float64)
    a_Intercept = unconstrained[0]
    b_Intercept = unconstrained[1]
    plate_argument_2 = unconstrained[2:4]
    def cell(ppl_cell_b_s_site_tau):
        constrain = jnp.exp(ppl_cell_b_s_site_tau)
        return constrain
    b_s_site_tau = jax.vmap(cell, in_axes=(0,))(plate_argument_2)
    plate_argument_3 = unconstrained[2:4]
    def cell_2(ppl_cell_b_s_site_tau):
        return ppl_cell_b_s_site_tau
    ppl_ljcells_b_s_site_tau = jax.vmap(cell_2, in_axes=(0,))(plate_argument_3)
    ppl_lkj_raw_b_s_site_L = unconstrained[4:5]
    ppl_lkj_partials_b_s_site_L = jnp.tanh(ppl_lkj_raw_b_s_site_L)
    out = jnp.ones((2,), dtype=jnp.float64)
    tuple_ = (1, 2, 1.0)
    i = tuple_[0]
    j = tuple_[1]
    d = tuple_[2]
    def loop_body(p, carry):
        (d_2, i_2, j_2, out_2) = carry
        d_3 = d_2 * jnp.sqrt(1 - ppl_lkj_partials_b_s_site_L[p - 1] ** 2)
        def then_arm():
            i_3 = i_2 + 1
            return (None, jnp.asarray(d_3, dtype=jnp.float64), jnp.asarray(i_3, dtype=jnp.int64), jnp.asarray(j_2, dtype=jnp.int64), out_2)
        def else_arm():
            out_3 = out_2.at[j_2 - 1].set(d_3)
            tuple__2 = (1, j_2 + 1, 1.0)
            i_4 = tuple__2[0]
            j_3 = tuple__2[1]
            d_4 = tuple__2[2]
            return (None, jnp.asarray(d_4, dtype=jnp.float64), jnp.asarray(i_4, dtype=jnp.int64), jnp.asarray(j_3, dtype=jnp.int64), out_3)
        branch = jax.lax.cond(i_2 + 1 < j_2, then_arm, else_arm)
        return (branch[1], branch[2], branch[3], branch[4])
    loop_carry = jax.lax.fori_loop(1, 2, loop_body, (jnp.asarray(d, dtype=jnp.float64), jnp.asarray(i, dtype=jnp.int64), jnp.asarray(j, dtype=jnp.int64), out))
    let_value = loop_carry[3]
    ppl_lkj_diagonal_b_s_site_L = let_value
    out_2 = jnp.zeros((2, 2), dtype=jnp.float64)
    out_3 = out_2.at[0, 0].set(1.0)
    tuple__2 = (1, 2, 1.0)
    i_2 = tuple__2[0]
    j_2 = tuple__2[1]
    w = tuple__2[2]
    def loop_body_2(p_2, carry_2):
        (i_3, j_3, out_4, w_2) = carry_2
        out_5 = out_4.at[j_3 - 1, i_3 - 1].set(ppl_lkj_partials_b_s_site_L[p_2 - 1] * w_2)
        w_3 = w_2 * jnp.sqrt(1 - ppl_lkj_partials_b_s_site_L[p_2 - 1] ** 2)
        def then_arm():
            i_4 = i_3 + 1
            return (None, jnp.asarray(i_4, dtype=jnp.int64), jnp.asarray(j_3, dtype=jnp.int64), out_5, jnp.asarray(w_3, dtype=jnp.float64))
        def else_arm():
            out_6 = out_5.at[j_3 - 1, j_3 - 1].set(ppl_lkj_diagonal_b_s_site_L[j_3 - 1])
            tuple__3 = (1, j_3 + 1, 1.0)
            i_5 = tuple__3[0]
            j_4 = tuple__3[1]
            w_4 = tuple__3[2]
            return (None, jnp.asarray(i_5, dtype=jnp.int64), jnp.asarray(j_4, dtype=jnp.int64), out_6, jnp.asarray(w_4, dtype=jnp.float64))
        branch = jax.lax.cond(i_3 + 1 < j_3, then_arm, else_arm)
        return (branch[1], branch[2], branch[3], branch[4])
    loop_carry_2 = jax.lax.fori_loop(1, 2, loop_body_2, (jnp.asarray(i_2, dtype=jnp.int64), jnp.asarray(j_2, dtype=jnp.int64), out_3, jnp.asarray(w, dtype=jnp.float64)))
    value = loop_carry_2[2]
    b_s_site_L = value
    total = 0.0
    tuple__3 = (1, 2)
    i_3 = tuple__3[0]
    j_3 = tuple__3[1]
    def loop_body_3(p_3, carry_3):
        (i_4, j_4, total_2) = carry_3
        total_3 = total_2 + (j_4 - i_4 + 1) / 2 * jnp.log(1 - ppl_lkj_partials_b_s_site_L[p_3 - 1] ** 2)
        def then_arm():
            return (i_4 + 1, j_4)
        def else_arm():
            return (1, j_4 + 1)
        tuple__4 = jax.lax.cond(i_4 + 1 < j_4, then_arm, else_arm)
        i_5 = tuple__4[0]
        j_5 = tuple__4[1]
        return (i_5, j_5, total_3)
    loop_carry_3 = jax.lax.fori_loop(1, 2, loop_body_3, (jnp.asarray(i_3, dtype=jnp.int64), jnp.asarray(j_3, dtype=jnp.int64), jnp.asarray(total, dtype=jnp.float64)))
    let_value_2 = loop_carry_3[2]
    ppl_lkj_logjac_b_s_site_L = let_value_2
    ppl_arrflat_b_s_site_z = unconstrained[5:ppl_last_b_s_site_z]
    b_s_site_z = jnp.reshape(ppl_arrflat_b_s_site_z, (ppl_axis1_b_s_site_z, 2), order="F")
    b_s_site = b_s_site_z @ jnp.transpose(b_s_site_tau[..., None] * b_s_site_L)
    ranef_a_s_site = b_s_site[site_level - 1, 0]
    ranef_b_s_site = b_s_site[site_level - 1, 1]
    a = a_Intercept + ranef_a_s_site
    b = b_Intercept + ranef_b_s_site
    def cell_3(ppl_pi_y_cell_argument_y_1, x_2, x_n, a_2, b_2):
        ppl_linear_x = x_2
        plate_value = jnp.exp(a_2[ppl_pi_y_cell_argument_y_1 - 1] + b_2[ppl_pi_y_cell_argument_y_1 - 1] * ppl_linear_x[ppl_pi_y_cell_argument_y_1 - 1])
        return (plate_value, x_n[ppl_pi_y_cell_argument_y_1 - 1])
    plate_value, plate_lengths = jax.vmap(cell_3, in_axes=(0, None, None, None, None))(plate_argument, x, x_lengths, a, b)
    y_cell_argument_y_1 = plate_value
    def cell_4(ppl_group_y, ppl_group_y_n, ppl_group_y_cell_argument_y_1, ppl_group_y_cell_argument_y_1_n):
        def cell_5(ppl_group_y_2, ppl_group_y_cell_argument_y_1_2):
            def then_arm():
                value_2 = ppl_group_y_2 >= 0
                def then_arm_2():
                    value_3 = ppl_group_y_2 == 0
                    def then_arm_3():
                        value_4 = -ppl_group_y_cell_argument_y_1_2
                        return value_4
                    def else_arm():
                        value_5 = jsp.gammaln(ppl_group_y_2 + 1.0)
                        value_6 = ppl_group_y_2 * jnp.log(ppl_group_y_cell_argument_y_1_2) - ppl_group_y_cell_argument_y_1_2 - value_5
                        return value_6
                    value_7 = jax.lax.cond(value_3, then_arm_3, else_arm)
                    return value_7
                def else_arm_2():
                    value_8 = -jnp.inf
                    return value_8
                value_9 = jax.lax.cond(value_2, then_arm_2, else_arm_2)
                return value_9
            def else_arm_3():
                return -jnp.inf
            ppl_density = jax.lax.cond(ppl_group_y_cell_argument_y_1_2 >= 0, then_arm, else_arm_3)
            return ppl_density
        ppl_pw_y_resp_group = jax.vmap(cell_5, in_axes=(0, 0))(ppl_group_y, ppl_group_y_cell_argument_y_1)
        ppl_group_total = jnp.sum(jnp.where((jnp.arange(ppl_pw_y_resp_group.shape[0]) < ppl_group_y_n), ppl_pw_y_resp_group, 0.0))
        return ppl_group_total
    ppl_pw_y_resp_totals = jax.vmap(cell_4, in_axes=(0, 0, 0, 0))(y, y_lengths, y_cell_argument_y_1, x_lengths[plate_argument - 1])
    ppl_lik_y_resp = jnp.sum(ppl_pw_y_resp_totals)
    plate_argument_4 = unconstrained[0:2]
    def cell_5(value_2, location, scale):
        log_scale = jnp.log(scale)
        def then_arm():
            return 1.0
        def else_arm():
            return scale
        standardized = (value_2 - location) / jax.lax.cond(jnp.logical_and(scale == 0, value_2 == location), then_arm, else_arm)
        standard_logpdf = -0.5 * np.log(2 * np.pi) - 0.5 * standardized ** 2
        def then_arm_2():
            return 0.0
        def else_arm_2():
            return log_scale
        logpdf = standard_logpdf - jax.lax.cond(jnp.logical_and(scale == 0, value_2 != location), then_arm_2, else_arm_2)
        return logpdf
    ppl_prior_parameters_normal_pw = jax.vmap(cell_5, in_axes=(0, 0, 0))(plate_argument_4, ppl_prior_parameters_normal_mu, ppl_prior_parameters_normal_sd)
    ppl_prior_parameters_normal = jnp.sum(ppl_prior_parameters_normal_pw)
    def cell_6(b_s_site_tau_2):
        scale_binding = 1.0
        log_scale = np.log(scale_binding)
        def then_arm():
            return -jnp.inf
        def else_arm():
            return -log_scale - b_s_site_tau_2 / scale_binding
        logpdf = jax.lax.cond(b_s_site_tau_2 < 0, then_arm, else_arm)
        return logpdf
    ppl_pw_prior_b_s_site_tau = jax.vmap(cell_6, in_axes=(0,))(b_s_site_tau)
    ppl_prior_b_s_site_tau = jnp.sum(ppl_pw_prior_b_s_site_tau)
    total_2 = -0.6931471805599453
    def loop_body_4(i_4, carry_4):
        (total_3,) = carry_4
        total_4 = total_3 + (2 - i_4) * jnp.log(ppl_lkj_diagonal_b_s_site_L[i_4 - 1])
        return (total_4,)
    loop_carry_4 = jax.lax.fori_loop(2, 3, loop_body_4, (jnp.asarray(total_2, dtype=jnp.float64),))
    let_value_3 = loop_carry_4[0]
    ppl_prior_b_s_site_L = let_value_3
    def cell_7(b_s_site_z_2):
        location_binding = 0.0
        scale_binding = 1.0
        log_scale = np.log(scale_binding)
        def then_arm():
            return b_s_site_z_2 == location_binding
        def else_arm():
            return False
        value_2 = (then_arm() if scale_binding == 0 else else_arm())
        def then_arm_2():
            return 1.0
        def else_arm_2():
            return scale_binding
        standardized = (b_s_site_z_2 - location_binding) / jax.lax.cond(value_2, then_arm_2, else_arm_2)
        standard_logpdf = -0.5 * np.log(2 * np.pi) - 0.5 * standardized ** 2
        def then_arm_3():
            return b_s_site_z_2 != location_binding
        def else_arm_3():
            return False
        value_3 = (then_arm_3() if scale_binding == 0 else else_arm_3())
        def then_arm_4():
            return 0.0
        def else_arm_4():
            return log_scale
        logpdf = standard_logpdf - jax.lax.cond(value_3, then_arm_4, else_arm_4)
        return logpdf
    ppl_pw_prior_b_s_site_z = jax.vmap(cell_7, in_axes=(0,))(ppl_arrflat_b_s_site_z)
    ppl_prior_b_s_site_z = jnp.sum(ppl_pw_prior_b_s_site_z)
    prior = ppl_prior_parameters_normal + ppl_prior_b_s_site_tau + ppl_prior_b_s_site_L + ppl_prior_b_s_site_z
    plate_fold = jnp.sum(ppl_ljcells_b_s_site_tau)
    log_jacobian = plate_fold + ppl_lkj_logjac_b_s_site_L
    posterior = prior + ppl_lik_y_resp + log_jacobian
    def then_arm():
        return None
    def else_arm():
        raise ValueError("[layout] packed length ≠ layout total: a packed point holds the coordinates of the binding whose layout packed it")
        return None
    value_2 = (then_arm() if unconstrained.size == ppl_last_b_s_site_z else else_arm())
    return posterior


def log_prior(unconstrained: ArrayLike, **prepared: ArrayLike) -> jax.Array:
    """The log prior density of the constrained parameters at `unconstrained`, the packed unconstrained parameters (length `dimension(prepared)`), for the data `prepare_data` returned: `f(u, **prepared)`."""
    ppl_last_b_s_site_z = prepared["ppl_last_b_s_site_z"]
    ppl_axis1_b_s_site_z = prepared["ppl_axis1_b_s_site_z"]
    site_level = prepared["site_level"]
    plate_argument = prepared["plate_argument"]
    x = prepared["x"]
    x_lengths = prepared["x_lengths"]
    x_flat = prepared["x_flat"]
    y = prepared["y"]
    y_lengths = prepared["y_lengths"]
    y_flat = prepared["y_flat"]
    unconstrained = jnp.asarray(unconstrained)
    site_level = jnp.asarray(site_level)
    plate_argument = jnp.asarray(plate_argument)
    x = jnp.asarray(x)
    x_lengths = jnp.asarray(x_lengths)
    x_flat = jnp.asarray(x_flat)
    y = jnp.asarray(y)
    y_lengths = jnp.asarray(y_lengths)
    y_flat = jnp.asarray(y_flat)
    ppl_prior_parameters_normal_mu = jnp.asarray([0.0, 0.0], dtype=jnp.float64)
    ppl_prior_parameters_normal_sd = jnp.asarray([1.0, 1.0], dtype=jnp.float64)
    plate_argument_2 = unconstrained[2:4]
    def cell(ppl_cell_b_s_site_tau):
        constrain = jnp.exp(ppl_cell_b_s_site_tau)
        return constrain
    b_s_site_tau = jax.vmap(cell, in_axes=(0,))(plate_argument_2)
    ppl_lkj_raw_b_s_site_L = unconstrained[4:5]
    ppl_lkj_partials_b_s_site_L = jnp.tanh(ppl_lkj_raw_b_s_site_L)
    out = jnp.ones((2,), dtype=jnp.float64)
    tuple_ = (1, 2, 1.0)
    i = tuple_[0]
    j = tuple_[1]
    d = tuple_[2]
    def loop_body(p, carry):
        (d_2, i_2, j_2, out_2) = carry
        d_3 = d_2 * jnp.sqrt(1 - ppl_lkj_partials_b_s_site_L[p - 1] ** 2)
        def then_arm():
            i_3 = i_2 + 1
            return (None, jnp.asarray(d_3, dtype=jnp.float64), jnp.asarray(i_3, dtype=jnp.int64), jnp.asarray(j_2, dtype=jnp.int64), out_2)
        def else_arm():
            out_3 = out_2.at[j_2 - 1].set(d_3)
            tuple__2 = (1, j_2 + 1, 1.0)
            i_4 = tuple__2[0]
            j_3 = tuple__2[1]
            d_4 = tuple__2[2]
            return (None, jnp.asarray(d_4, dtype=jnp.float64), jnp.asarray(i_4, dtype=jnp.int64), jnp.asarray(j_3, dtype=jnp.int64), out_3)
        branch = jax.lax.cond(i_2 + 1 < j_2, then_arm, else_arm)
        return (branch[1], branch[2], branch[3], branch[4])
    loop_carry = jax.lax.fori_loop(1, 2, loop_body, (jnp.asarray(d, dtype=jnp.float64), jnp.asarray(i, dtype=jnp.int64), jnp.asarray(j, dtype=jnp.int64), out))
    let_value = loop_carry[3]
    ppl_lkj_diagonal_b_s_site_L = let_value
    ppl_arrflat_b_s_site_z = unconstrained[5:ppl_last_b_s_site_z]
    plate_argument_3 = unconstrained[0:2]
    def cell_2(value, location, scale):
        log_scale = jnp.log(scale)
        def then_arm():
            return 1.0
        def else_arm():
            return scale
        standardized = (value - location) / jax.lax.cond(jnp.logical_and(scale == 0, value == location), then_arm, else_arm)
        standard_logpdf = -0.5 * np.log(2 * np.pi) - 0.5 * standardized ** 2
        def then_arm_2():
            return 0.0
        def else_arm_2():
            return log_scale
        logpdf = standard_logpdf - jax.lax.cond(jnp.logical_and(scale == 0, value != location), then_arm_2, else_arm_2)
        return logpdf
    ppl_prior_parameters_normal_pw = jax.vmap(cell_2, in_axes=(0, 0, 0))(plate_argument_3, ppl_prior_parameters_normal_mu, ppl_prior_parameters_normal_sd)
    ppl_prior_parameters_normal = jnp.sum(ppl_prior_parameters_normal_pw)
    def cell_3(b_s_site_tau_2):
        scale_binding = 1.0
        log_scale = np.log(scale_binding)
        def then_arm():
            return -jnp.inf
        def else_arm():
            return -log_scale - b_s_site_tau_2 / scale_binding
        logpdf = jax.lax.cond(b_s_site_tau_2 < 0, then_arm, else_arm)
        return logpdf
    ppl_pw_prior_b_s_site_tau = jax.vmap(cell_3, in_axes=(0,))(b_s_site_tau)
    ppl_prior_b_s_site_tau = jnp.sum(ppl_pw_prior_b_s_site_tau)
    total = -0.6931471805599453
    def loop_body_2(i_2, carry_2):
        (total_2,) = carry_2
        total_3 = total_2 + (2 - i_2) * jnp.log(ppl_lkj_diagonal_b_s_site_L[i_2 - 1])
        return (total_3,)
    loop_carry_2 = jax.lax.fori_loop(2, 3, loop_body_2, (jnp.asarray(total, dtype=jnp.float64),))
    let_value_2 = loop_carry_2[0]
    ppl_prior_b_s_site_L = let_value_2
    def cell_4(b_s_site_z):
        location_binding = 0.0
        scale_binding = 1.0
        log_scale = np.log(scale_binding)
        def then_arm():
            return b_s_site_z == location_binding
        def else_arm():
            return False
        value = (then_arm() if scale_binding == 0 else else_arm())
        def then_arm_2():
            return 1.0
        def else_arm_2():
            return scale_binding
        standardized = (b_s_site_z - location_binding) / jax.lax.cond(value, then_arm_2, else_arm_2)
        standard_logpdf = -0.5 * np.log(2 * np.pi) - 0.5 * standardized ** 2
        def then_arm_3():
            return b_s_site_z != location_binding
        def else_arm_3():
            return False
        value_2 = (then_arm_3() if scale_binding == 0 else else_arm_3())
        def then_arm_4():
            return 0.0
        def else_arm_4():
            return log_scale
        logpdf = standard_logpdf - jax.lax.cond(value_2, then_arm_4, else_arm_4)
        return logpdf
    ppl_pw_prior_b_s_site_z = jax.vmap(cell_4, in_axes=(0,))(ppl_arrflat_b_s_site_z)
    ppl_prior_b_s_site_z = jnp.sum(ppl_pw_prior_b_s_site_z)
    prior = ppl_prior_parameters_normal + ppl_prior_b_s_site_tau + ppl_prior_b_s_site_L + ppl_prior_b_s_site_z
    def then_arm():
        return None
    def else_arm():
        raise ValueError("[layout] packed length ≠ layout total: a packed point holds the coordinates of the binding whose layout packed it")
        return None
    value = (then_arm() if unconstrained.size == ppl_last_b_s_site_z else else_arm())
    return prior


def log_likelihood(unconstrained: ArrayLike, **prepared: ArrayLike) -> jax.Array:
    """The log likelihood of the observations at `unconstrained`, the packed unconstrained parameters (length `dimension(prepared)`), for the data `prepare_data` returned: `f(u, **prepared)`."""
    ppl_last_b_s_site_z = prepared["ppl_last_b_s_site_z"]
    ppl_axis1_b_s_site_z = prepared["ppl_axis1_b_s_site_z"]
    site_level = prepared["site_level"]
    plate_argument = prepared["plate_argument"]
    x = prepared["x"]
    x_lengths = prepared["x_lengths"]
    x_flat = prepared["x_flat"]
    y = prepared["y"]
    y_lengths = prepared["y_lengths"]
    y_flat = prepared["y_flat"]
    unconstrained = jnp.asarray(unconstrained)
    site_level = jnp.asarray(site_level)
    plate_argument = jnp.asarray(plate_argument)
    x = jnp.asarray(x)
    x_lengths = jnp.asarray(x_lengths)
    x_flat = jnp.asarray(x_flat)
    y = jnp.asarray(y)
    y_lengths = jnp.asarray(y_lengths)
    y_flat = jnp.asarray(y_flat)
    a_Intercept = unconstrained[0]
    b_Intercept = unconstrained[1]
    plate_argument_2 = unconstrained[2:4]
    def cell(ppl_cell_b_s_site_tau):
        constrain = jnp.exp(ppl_cell_b_s_site_tau)
        return constrain
    b_s_site_tau = jax.vmap(cell, in_axes=(0,))(plate_argument_2)
    ppl_lkj_raw_b_s_site_L = unconstrained[4:5]
    ppl_lkj_partials_b_s_site_L = jnp.tanh(ppl_lkj_raw_b_s_site_L)
    out = jnp.ones((2,), dtype=jnp.float64)
    tuple_ = (1, 2, 1.0)
    i = tuple_[0]
    j = tuple_[1]
    d = tuple_[2]
    def loop_body(p, carry):
        (d_2, i_2, j_2, out_2) = carry
        d_3 = d_2 * jnp.sqrt(1 - ppl_lkj_partials_b_s_site_L[p - 1] ** 2)
        def then_arm():
            i_3 = i_2 + 1
            return (None, jnp.asarray(d_3, dtype=jnp.float64), jnp.asarray(i_3, dtype=jnp.int64), jnp.asarray(j_2, dtype=jnp.int64), out_2)
        def else_arm():
            out_3 = out_2.at[j_2 - 1].set(d_3)
            tuple__2 = (1, j_2 + 1, 1.0)
            i_4 = tuple__2[0]
            j_3 = tuple__2[1]
            d_4 = tuple__2[2]
            return (None, jnp.asarray(d_4, dtype=jnp.float64), jnp.asarray(i_4, dtype=jnp.int64), jnp.asarray(j_3, dtype=jnp.int64), out_3)
        branch = jax.lax.cond(i_2 + 1 < j_2, then_arm, else_arm)
        return (branch[1], branch[2], branch[3], branch[4])
    loop_carry = jax.lax.fori_loop(1, 2, loop_body, (jnp.asarray(d, dtype=jnp.float64), jnp.asarray(i, dtype=jnp.int64), jnp.asarray(j, dtype=jnp.int64), out))
    let_value = loop_carry[3]
    ppl_lkj_diagonal_b_s_site_L = let_value
    out_2 = jnp.zeros((2, 2), dtype=jnp.float64)
    out_3 = out_2.at[0, 0].set(1.0)
    tuple__2 = (1, 2, 1.0)
    i_2 = tuple__2[0]
    j_2 = tuple__2[1]
    w = tuple__2[2]
    def loop_body_2(p_2, carry_2):
        (i_3, j_3, out_4, w_2) = carry_2
        out_5 = out_4.at[j_3 - 1, i_3 - 1].set(ppl_lkj_partials_b_s_site_L[p_2 - 1] * w_2)
        w_3 = w_2 * jnp.sqrt(1 - ppl_lkj_partials_b_s_site_L[p_2 - 1] ** 2)
        def then_arm():
            i_4 = i_3 + 1
            return (None, jnp.asarray(i_4, dtype=jnp.int64), jnp.asarray(j_3, dtype=jnp.int64), out_5, jnp.asarray(w_3, dtype=jnp.float64))
        def else_arm():
            out_6 = out_5.at[j_3 - 1, j_3 - 1].set(ppl_lkj_diagonal_b_s_site_L[j_3 - 1])
            tuple__3 = (1, j_3 + 1, 1.0)
            i_5 = tuple__3[0]
            j_4 = tuple__3[1]
            w_4 = tuple__3[2]
            return (None, jnp.asarray(i_5, dtype=jnp.int64), jnp.asarray(j_4, dtype=jnp.int64), out_6, jnp.asarray(w_4, dtype=jnp.float64))
        branch = jax.lax.cond(i_3 + 1 < j_3, then_arm, else_arm)
        return (branch[1], branch[2], branch[3], branch[4])
    loop_carry_2 = jax.lax.fori_loop(1, 2, loop_body_2, (jnp.asarray(i_2, dtype=jnp.int64), jnp.asarray(j_2, dtype=jnp.int64), out_3, jnp.asarray(w, dtype=jnp.float64)))
    value = loop_carry_2[2]
    b_s_site_L = value
    ppl_arrflat_b_s_site_z = unconstrained[5:ppl_last_b_s_site_z]
    b_s_site_z = jnp.reshape(ppl_arrflat_b_s_site_z, (ppl_axis1_b_s_site_z, 2), order="F")
    b_s_site = b_s_site_z @ jnp.transpose(b_s_site_tau[..., None] * b_s_site_L)
    ranef_a_s_site = b_s_site[site_level - 1, 0]
    ranef_b_s_site = b_s_site[site_level - 1, 1]
    a = a_Intercept + ranef_a_s_site
    b = b_Intercept + ranef_b_s_site
    def cell_2(ppl_pi_y_cell_argument_y_1, x_2, x_n, a_2, b_2):
        ppl_linear_x = x_2
        plate_value = jnp.exp(a_2[ppl_pi_y_cell_argument_y_1 - 1] + b_2[ppl_pi_y_cell_argument_y_1 - 1] * ppl_linear_x[ppl_pi_y_cell_argument_y_1 - 1])
        return (plate_value, x_n[ppl_pi_y_cell_argument_y_1 - 1])
    plate_value, plate_lengths = jax.vmap(cell_2, in_axes=(0, None, None, None, None))(plate_argument, x, x_lengths, a, b)
    y_cell_argument_y_1 = plate_value
    def cell_3(ppl_group_y, ppl_group_y_n, ppl_group_y_cell_argument_y_1, ppl_group_y_cell_argument_y_1_n):
        def cell_4(ppl_group_y_2, ppl_group_y_cell_argument_y_1_2):
            def then_arm():
                value_2 = ppl_group_y_2 >= 0
                def then_arm_2():
                    value_3 = ppl_group_y_2 == 0
                    def then_arm_3():
                        value_4 = -ppl_group_y_cell_argument_y_1_2
                        return value_4
                    def else_arm():
                        value_5 = jsp.gammaln(ppl_group_y_2 + 1.0)
                        value_6 = ppl_group_y_2 * jnp.log(ppl_group_y_cell_argument_y_1_2) - ppl_group_y_cell_argument_y_1_2 - value_5
                        return value_6
                    value_7 = jax.lax.cond(value_3, then_arm_3, else_arm)
                    return value_7
                def else_arm_2():
                    value_8 = -jnp.inf
                    return value_8
                value_9 = jax.lax.cond(value_2, then_arm_2, else_arm_2)
                return value_9
            def else_arm_3():
                return -jnp.inf
            ppl_density = jax.lax.cond(ppl_group_y_cell_argument_y_1_2 >= 0, then_arm, else_arm_3)
            return ppl_density
        ppl_pw_y_resp_group = jax.vmap(cell_4, in_axes=(0, 0))(ppl_group_y, ppl_group_y_cell_argument_y_1)
        ppl_group_total = jnp.sum(jnp.where((jnp.arange(ppl_pw_y_resp_group.shape[0]) < ppl_group_y_n), ppl_pw_y_resp_group, 0.0))
        return ppl_group_total
    ppl_pw_y_resp_totals = jax.vmap(cell_3, in_axes=(0, 0, 0, 0))(y, y_lengths, y_cell_argument_y_1, x_lengths[plate_argument - 1])
    ppl_lik_y_resp = jnp.sum(ppl_pw_y_resp_totals)
    def then_arm():
        return None
    def else_arm():
        raise ValueError("[layout] packed length ≠ layout total: a packed point holds the coordinates of the binding whose layout packed it")
        return None
    value_2 = (then_arm() if unconstrained.size == ppl_last_b_s_site_z else else_arm())
    return ppl_lik_y_resp


def log_jacobian(unconstrained: ArrayLike, **prepared: ArrayLike) -> jax.Array:
    """The log Jacobian of the constraining transforms at `unconstrained`, the packed unconstrained parameters (length `dimension(prepared)`), for the data `prepare_data` returned: `f(u, **prepared)`."""
    ppl_last_b_s_site_z = prepared["ppl_last_b_s_site_z"]
    ppl_axis1_b_s_site_z = prepared["ppl_axis1_b_s_site_z"]
    site_level = prepared["site_level"]
    plate_argument = prepared["plate_argument"]
    x = prepared["x"]
    x_lengths = prepared["x_lengths"]
    x_flat = prepared["x_flat"]
    y = prepared["y"]
    y_lengths = prepared["y_lengths"]
    y_flat = prepared["y_flat"]
    unconstrained = jnp.asarray(unconstrained)
    site_level = jnp.asarray(site_level)
    plate_argument = jnp.asarray(plate_argument)
    x = jnp.asarray(x)
    x_lengths = jnp.asarray(x_lengths)
    x_flat = jnp.asarray(x_flat)
    y = jnp.asarray(y)
    y_lengths = jnp.asarray(y_lengths)
    y_flat = jnp.asarray(y_flat)
    plate_argument_2 = unconstrained[2:4]
    def cell(ppl_cell_b_s_site_tau):
        return ppl_cell_b_s_site_tau
    ppl_ljcells_b_s_site_tau = jax.vmap(cell, in_axes=(0,))(plate_argument_2)
    ppl_lkj_raw_b_s_site_L = unconstrained[4:5]
    ppl_lkj_partials_b_s_site_L = jnp.tanh(ppl_lkj_raw_b_s_site_L)
    total = 0.0
    tuple_ = (1, 2)
    i = tuple_[0]
    j = tuple_[1]
    def loop_body(p, carry):
        (i_2, j_2, total_2) = carry
        total_3 = total_2 + (j_2 - i_2 + 1) / 2 * jnp.log(1 - ppl_lkj_partials_b_s_site_L[p - 1] ** 2)
        def then_arm():
            return (i_2 + 1, j_2)
        def else_arm():
            return (1, j_2 + 1)
        tuple__2 = jax.lax.cond(i_2 + 1 < j_2, then_arm, else_arm)
        i_3 = tuple__2[0]
        j_3 = tuple__2[1]
        return (i_3, j_3, total_3)
    loop_carry = jax.lax.fori_loop(1, 2, loop_body, (jnp.asarray(i, dtype=jnp.int64), jnp.asarray(j, dtype=jnp.int64), jnp.asarray(total, dtype=jnp.float64)))
    let_value = loop_carry[2]
    ppl_lkj_logjac_b_s_site_L = let_value
    plate_fold = jnp.sum(ppl_ljcells_b_s_site_tau)
    log_jacobian_2 = plate_fold + ppl_lkj_logjac_b_s_site_L
    def then_arm():
        return None
    def else_arm():
        raise ValueError("[layout] packed length ≠ layout total: a packed point holds the coordinates of the binding whose layout packed it")
        return None
    value = (then_arm() if unconstrained.size == ppl_last_b_s_site_z else else_arm())
    return log_jacobian_2


def constrain(unconstrained: ArrayLike, **prepared: ArrayLike) -> dict:
    """The constrained value of each parameter, and of each requested value, at `unconstrained`, the packed unconstrained parameters (length `dimension(prepared)`), for the data `prepare_data` returned: `f(u, **prepared)`. Returns a dict, a submodel's values nested under its name:

    - `a_Intercept`
    - `b_Intercept`
    - `b_s_site.tau`
    - `b_s_site.L`
    - `b_s_site.z`
    """
    ppl_last_b_s_site_z = prepared["ppl_last_b_s_site_z"]
    ppl_axis1_b_s_site_z = prepared["ppl_axis1_b_s_site_z"]
    site_level = prepared["site_level"]
    plate_argument = prepared["plate_argument"]
    x = prepared["x"]
    x_lengths = prepared["x_lengths"]
    x_flat = prepared["x_flat"]
    y = prepared["y"]
    y_lengths = prepared["y_lengths"]
    y_flat = prepared["y_flat"]
    unconstrained = jnp.asarray(unconstrained)
    site_level = jnp.asarray(site_level)
    plate_argument = jnp.asarray(plate_argument)
    x = jnp.asarray(x)
    x_lengths = jnp.asarray(x_lengths)
    x_flat = jnp.asarray(x_flat)
    y = jnp.asarray(y)
    y_lengths = jnp.asarray(y_lengths)
    y_flat = jnp.asarray(y_flat)
    a_Intercept = unconstrained[0]
    b_Intercept = unconstrained[1]
    plate_argument_2 = unconstrained[2:4]
    def cell(ppl_cell_b_s_site_tau):
        constrain_2 = jnp.exp(ppl_cell_b_s_site_tau)
        return constrain_2
    b_s_site_tau = jax.vmap(cell, in_axes=(0,))(plate_argument_2)
    ppl_lkj_raw_b_s_site_L = unconstrained[4:5]
    ppl_lkj_partials_b_s_site_L = jnp.tanh(ppl_lkj_raw_b_s_site_L)
    out = jnp.ones((2,), dtype=jnp.float64)
    tuple_ = (1, 2, 1.0)
    i = tuple_[0]
    j = tuple_[1]
    d = tuple_[2]
    def loop_body(p, carry):
        (d_2, i_2, j_2, out_2) = carry
        d_3 = d_2 * jnp.sqrt(1 - ppl_lkj_partials_b_s_site_L[p - 1] ** 2)
        def then_arm():
            i_3 = i_2 + 1
            return (None, jnp.asarray(d_3, dtype=jnp.float64), jnp.asarray(i_3, dtype=jnp.int64), jnp.asarray(j_2, dtype=jnp.int64), out_2)
        def else_arm():
            out_3 = out_2.at[j_2 - 1].set(d_3)
            tuple__2 = (1, j_2 + 1, 1.0)
            i_4 = tuple__2[0]
            j_3 = tuple__2[1]
            d_4 = tuple__2[2]
            return (None, jnp.asarray(d_4, dtype=jnp.float64), jnp.asarray(i_4, dtype=jnp.int64), jnp.asarray(j_3, dtype=jnp.int64), out_3)
        branch = jax.lax.cond(i_2 + 1 < j_2, then_arm, else_arm)
        return (branch[1], branch[2], branch[3], branch[4])
    loop_carry = jax.lax.fori_loop(1, 2, loop_body, (jnp.asarray(d, dtype=jnp.float64), jnp.asarray(i, dtype=jnp.int64), jnp.asarray(j, dtype=jnp.int64), out))
    let_value = loop_carry[3]
    ppl_lkj_diagonal_b_s_site_L = let_value
    out_2 = jnp.zeros((2, 2), dtype=jnp.float64)
    out_3 = out_2.at[0, 0].set(1.0)
    tuple__2 = (1, 2, 1.0)
    i_2 = tuple__2[0]
    j_2 = tuple__2[1]
    w = tuple__2[2]
    def loop_body_2(p_2, carry_2):
        (i_3, j_3, out_4, w_2) = carry_2
        out_5 = out_4.at[j_3 - 1, i_3 - 1].set(ppl_lkj_partials_b_s_site_L[p_2 - 1] * w_2)
        w_3 = w_2 * jnp.sqrt(1 - ppl_lkj_partials_b_s_site_L[p_2 - 1] ** 2)
        def then_arm():
            i_4 = i_3 + 1
            return (None, jnp.asarray(i_4, dtype=jnp.int64), jnp.asarray(j_3, dtype=jnp.int64), out_5, jnp.asarray(w_3, dtype=jnp.float64))
        def else_arm():
            out_6 = out_5.at[j_3 - 1, j_3 - 1].set(ppl_lkj_diagonal_b_s_site_L[j_3 - 1])
            tuple__3 = (1, j_3 + 1, 1.0)
            i_5 = tuple__3[0]
            j_4 = tuple__3[1]
            w_4 = tuple__3[2]
            return (None, jnp.asarray(i_5, dtype=jnp.int64), jnp.asarray(j_4, dtype=jnp.int64), out_6, jnp.asarray(w_4, dtype=jnp.float64))
        branch = jax.lax.cond(i_3 + 1 < j_3, then_arm, else_arm)
        return (branch[1], branch[2], branch[3], branch[4])
    loop_carry_2 = jax.lax.fori_loop(1, 2, loop_body_2, (jnp.asarray(i_2, dtype=jnp.int64), jnp.asarray(j_2, dtype=jnp.int64), out_3, jnp.asarray(w, dtype=jnp.float64)))
    value = loop_carry_2[2]
    b_s_site_L = value
    ppl_arrflat_b_s_site_z = unconstrained[5:ppl_last_b_s_site_z]
    b_s_site_z = jnp.reshape(ppl_arrflat_b_s_site_z, (ppl_axis1_b_s_site_z, 2), order="F")
    def then_arm():
        return None
    def else_arm():
        raise ValueError("[layout] packed length ≠ layout total: a packed point holds the coordinates of the binding whose layout packed it")
        return None
    value_2 = (then_arm() if unconstrained.size == ppl_last_b_s_site_z else else_arm())
    return {"a_Intercept": a_Intercept, "b_Intercept": b_Intercept, "b_s_site": {"tau": b_s_site_tau, "L": b_s_site_L, "z": b_s_site_z}}


def dimension(prepared: dict) -> int:
    """The length of `unconstrained` for the data `prepared` (what `prepare_data` returned)."""
    return int(prepared["ppl_last_b_s_site_z"])


def parameters(prepared: dict) -> dict:
    """Each parameter's 0-based `start`/`stop` in `unconstrained`, its transform and dimensions,
    for the data `prepared` (what `prepare_data` returned)."""
    return {"a_Intercept": {"start": 0, "stop": 1, "transform": "identity", "dims": ()}, "b_Intercept": {"start": 1, "stop": 2, "transform": "identity", "dims": ()}, "b_s_site.tau": {"start": 2, "stop": 4, "transform": "exp", "dims": (2,)}, "b_s_site.L": {"start": 4, "stop": 5, "transform": "lkj", "dims": (2, 2)}, "b_s_site.z": {"start": 5, "stop": int(prepared["ppl_last_b_s_site_z"]), "transform": "identity", "dims": (int(prepared["ppl_axis1_b_s_site_z"]), 2)}}

The fits below use 400 sites with 3200 counts in all. The posterior has 805 coordinates: the two population intercepts, the two random-effect sds, the correlation, and two standardized effects per site.

Fit the model on minibatches ​

brm_svi_fit takes the model on the ReactiveKernels backend, RKBRMI(brmi), with Enzyme loaded for the gradients. Its units are those identified by brm_nlme_view: here one site with its visits. In this model, every observation belongs to exactly one unit, which is what makes a batch of units an unbiased estimate of the whole likelihood.

The approximation has two parts. The coordinates every unit reads, here the population intercepts, the two sds and the correlation, are the globals; their factor is a diagonal Gaussian (family=MeanFieldGlobal(), the default) or a diagonal plus a low-rank part (LowRankGlobal(r)). Each unit's own coordinates, here a site's two standardized effects, get a full Gaussian of their own. The family has no dependence between a unit and the globals, or between two units.

One iteration draws the globals once and a batch of 32 sites. With the default unit_curvature=:orthogonal, each selected two-coordinate site is evaluated at two orthogonal antithetic pairs, giving four unit gradients. Adam updates the global factor and a natural-gradient step updates each selected site's factor. Sites not in the batch do not move. The NUTS reference uses rk_logdensity_problem through WarmupHMC's quorum sampler: four chains are requested, with at least three required to finish. Each starts from its own jittered point near the origin of the unconstrained coordinates.

Quorum selection can favour faster chains; the retained-chain diagnostics cannot rule out that selection bias.

These fitting calls run in the test environment and produce the cached results; the documentation build does not sample.

julia
# SVI: one call. The units are the sites; each iteration draws a batch of 32 of them.
fit_svi(rk; seed=1, progress=nothing) =
    brm_svi_fit(rk; batch_size=32, rng=Xoshiro(seed), ndraws=2000, progress)

# NUTS: four adaptive chains of WarmupHMC on the same log density, with a quorum
# of three completed chains and at least 4000 draws each after warm-up.
# Run Julia with one thread for these Enzyme gradients. Each chain starts near the origin of the
# unconstrained coordinates (unit random-effect sds, no site effects), jittered per
# chain, with a unit diagonal metric.
function fit_nuts(rk; seed=10, chains=4, n_draws=4000, progress=nothing)
    problem = rk_logdensity_problem(rk; ad_backend=AutoEnzyme(; mode=Enzyme.Reverse))
    d = LogDensityProblems.dimension(problem)
    rngs = [Xoshiro(seed + c) for c in 1:chains]
    init = [(; position=0.1 .* randn(rng, d), squared_scale=ones(d)) for rng in rngs]
    completion_warmup_mcmc(rngs, problem;
        init, n_draws, min_completed=max(1, div(3chains, 4)), progress).results
end

Both return draws in the packed coordinates of the RK model, one column per draw, so the same readers take them. rk_restore_draws(rk, draws) restores the named parameters on their natural scale; the random-effect block comes back as its margin sds, the Cholesky factor of their correlation and the standardized site draws:

julia
# The population parameters of `site_counts_model`, read from restored draws, each as
# a vector over draws on its natural scale. The random-effect block `s` of the two
# predictors restores as `b_s_site`: `tau` (margin sds, 2 × draws), `L` (one lower
# Cholesky factor of the margins' correlation per draw) and `z` (one sites × 2 matrix
# of standardized site draws per draw). Margin 1 is `a`, margin 2 is `b`.
function population(restored)
    block = restored.b_s_site
    (; a=restored.a_Intercept, b=restored.b_Intercept,
       sd_a=block.tau[1, :], sd_b=block.tau[2, :],
       cor_ab=[(L * L')[1, 2] for L in block.L])
end

# Each site's intercept and slope deviation from the population values, sites × draws.
function site_effects(restored)
    block = restored.b_s_site
    E = [block.tau[:, k] .* (block.L[k] * block.z[k]') for k in eachindex(block.L)]
    (; a=reduce(hcat, (e[1, :] for e in E)), b=reduce(hcat, (e[2, :] for e in E)))
end

How the fit stops ​

The optimization runs in windows of about N / B iterations, at least 100, and stops on its own in three phases. Exploring steps the globals at a constant rate until a window's 10 % trimmed ELBO mean has stopped rising, with no upward trend over the last windows. Settling steps by a fraction of each global's own scale, until the same test passes again or its window limit is reached. Polishing decays the step over 20 windows and returns the average of its iterates. diagnostics.converged reports that the rule fired, which is not a proof that a slow drift has settled.

The fit ran 7700 iterations in 46 windows exploring, 11 windows settling and 20 windows polishing, evaluating 985600 site gradients, in 3.2 min. The 3 retained NUTS chains took 3.1 min for 12000 draws (249408 gradient evaluations after warm-up): maximum R-hat 1.003, minimum bulk ESS 1558, minimum tail ESS 3471, 0 divergent transitions (0 % of retained draws). Both ran on one CPU of a shared host; the times are what that run took, not a benchmark.

Continue the fit as data arrive ​

A fitted approximation is a data-free summary of what was seen. brm_svi_checkpoint(fit) returns it as a BRMSVIState: the global factor, one factor per unit keyed by the unit's label, and the frozen preprocessing of the model. It holds no observations and no compiled model, so it serializes and travels. brm_svi_fit(state, builder(window)) then fits a new window of observations with that state as the prior and the start: the target is q_previous(globals, units) * p(window | globals, units).

Units are matched by label. A site seen before continues from its carried factor; a site that is new in a window starts from its authored prior under the carried global factor; a site absent from a window keeps its factor in the state and recovers it when it returns. Each observation enters once: the history is represented by the carried Gaussians and never revisited, so a stream of windows is a sequential approximation that can depend on the order of the windows and need not equal a fit of everything at once.

The stream here cuts the eight visits into four windows of two. The windows contain 300, 340, 380 and 400 sites. A site contributes observations only after it arrives; its earlier visits are absent from the accumulated reference data too:

julia
# Window `w` holds visits `2w - 1` and `2w` of the sites present in it. Sites arrive
# over time: 300 in the first window, then 340, 380 and 400.
function stream_windows(data; n_windows=4, first_sites=300, new_sites_per_window=40)
    visits = length(first(data.x))
    per_window = visits ÷ n_windows
    map(1:n_windows) do w
        n = min(length(data.site), first_sites + (w - 1) * new_sites_per_window)
        range = ((w - 1) * per_window + 1):(w * per_window)
        (; site=data.site[1:n], x=[xi[range] for xi in data.x[1:n]], y=[yi[range] for yi in data.y[1:n]])
    end
end
julia
# The stream: calibrate on the first window, then continue through the others,
# carrying only the checkpoint from one window to the next.
function stream_fits(windows; progress=nothing)
    fit = brm_svi_fit(site_counts_model(windows[1]); batch_size=32, rng=Xoshiro(1), progress)
    fits = Any[fit] # a continuation carries a BRMSVIState where the initial fit has nothing
    for w in 2:length(windows)
        state = brm_svi_checkpoint(fit)
        fit = brm_svi_fit(state, site_counts_model(windows[w]); batch_size=32, rng=Xoshiro(w), progress)
        push!(fits, fit)
    end
    fits
end

After each window, the streaming estimate of every population parameter is compared with two fits of all the data seen up to that window: NUTS and a batch brm_svi_fit.

Each update minibatches only the units of its window; the checkpoint grows with the number of distinct sites. The narrow global sds carry over, and the sequential approximation also drifts away from the accumulated-data posterior.

At the final window the correlation mean is 3.7 NUTS posterior sds from its NUTS estimate. Across the window references, maximum R-hat is 1.005, minimum bulk/tail ESS are 888/1083, and 0 retained transitions diverged.

Start from fits on slices of the data ​

brm_svi_combine turns checkpoints from disjoint slices of units into a start for the full fit. The global factors multiply as Gaussian sites with the prior counted once; each unit takes its slice's factor. The full model remains the target. Name parameters in exclude when their meaning changes between a slice and the full model; those parameters start cold.

julia
# Fits of four slices of the sites, their checkpoints combined into a start for the
# full fit. `full` is prepared once, so the combination and the fit share it.
function combined_start_fit(data, slices; progress=nothing)
    slice_fits = [brm_svi_fit(site_counts_model(slice); batch_size=32, rng=Xoshiro(s), progress)
                  for (s, slice) in enumerate(slices)]
    full = brm_nlme_model(RKBRMI(site_counts_model(data)); ad_backend=AutoEnzyme(; mode=Enzyme.Reverse))
    start = brm_svi_combine(full, brm_svi_checkpoint.(slice_fits))
    started = brm_svi_fit(full; start, batch_size=32, rng=Xoshiro(1), ndraws=2000, progress)
    (; full, start, started)
end
start of the full fitoptimizationtotal including slicesiterationsof them exploringsite gradientslargest population mean error (NUTS sds)
cold start44 s3.2 min770046009856000.36
start from four slices11 s74 s710040009088000.28

The combined start shortens exploration here. Total times include preparation and compilation, depend on run order, and are not a speed benchmark.

Two-stage fits and Laplace ​

brm_two_stage_fit, through the NLMEEstimation extension, estimates the population by SAEM and samples each unit's conditional posterior at those fixed estimates. It does not propagate population uncertainty.

brm_svi_fit with laplace=GaussianBlockLaplace() integrates a shared Gaussian block jointly with Gaussian population coefficients and unit effects, conditional on the remaining variational globals. It requires affine Gaussian likelihoods and visits the whole window at each update.

Reproduce

The research recipe contains the synthetic data generator, fit commands, cache rules and figure source. Fits run once locally; the documentation build renders their summary tables and AlgebraOfVega specifications. It never samples.
